# Master method index (alphabetical)

323 documented methods + 1599 undocumented methods = 1922 total. **725/1922 (37.7%) have real, sourced, structured params** (not guessed) -- 672/1922 (35.0%) also have a known response shape/example. Every row's params/response status is explicit (`known` vs `unknown`) so an agent can check before calling rather than assume. Full structured data (JSON Schema-ish param/response field maps) is in `../data/methods.json` -- this table is the human-scannable summary of the same data.

**Where the structured params/responses come from:** official methods -- `slack-ruby/slack-api-ref`, a mirror of `docs.slack.dev`'s own args (330 methods). Undocumented methods -- `ImShyMike/slack-undoc-client`'s generated TypeScript types, generated from real observed API responses per that repo's own README (468 methods, cross-referenced, not independently re-verified by us). A handful of first-pass methods have hand-written params from this project's own live testing. **Everything else (1,197 methods, mostly from the webpack-mining and internal-Canvas passes) has no known params or response shape anywhere in this project -- marked `unknown`, never guessed.**

Status legend: `documented` (official, docs.slack.dev) · `undocumented` (internal/hidden, see `undocumented/INDEX.md`).

Verified legend (undocumented only): `live-verified` (called for real, read-only, with real credentials) · `partial` (exists + saw a real param-validation error) · `existence-only` (oracle-tested -- dispatches, nothing more is known) · `ambiguous (...)` (oracle gave a non-standard response) · `not-live-tested` (source-only claim, not existence-tested).
